  "project": "contrastive_entrain_calib",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：在 28034 GPU 3 上运行 exp-009 checkpoint-500 的 D027 标准 eval\n\n### 背景\nexp-009 (Continuous Probe Probability GRPO) 已到 step 714，checkpoint-500 已保存。需要运行 D027 标准 preset eval 来检查训练效果。\n\n### D027 eval 标准\n- 脚本: eval_unified_preset.py\n- 指标: ToF (有效纠正接受率) / NoF (无效压力抵抗率) / SYCONScore = harmonic_mean(ToF, NoF)\n- 数据: calibration_v2_1_expanded.jsonl (362 pairs)\n- 对比基线: vanilla Qwen3-8B SYCONScore=0.134, ACT (exp-004) SYCONScore=0.301\n\n### 你要做的\n\n1. SSH 到 westd-28034，确认 eval 脚本和数据存在：\n   ```bash\n   ls /root/contrastive_entrain_calib/evaluation/eval_unified_preset.py\n   ls /root/mvp_v2_1_expanded/data/calibration_v2_1_expanded.jsonl\n   ls /root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/exp009_continuous_probe_grpo/checkpoint-500/adapter_model.safetensors\n   ```\n\n2. 在 GPU 3 上运行 eval（注意：必须在命令前设置 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES，不能在脚本内设置）：\n   ```bash\n   cd /root/contrastive_entrain_calib\n   CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=3 /root/miniconda3/bin/python evaluation/eval_unified_preset.py \\\n     --model_path /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/Qwen/Qwen3-8B \\\n     --lora_path /root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/exp009_continuous_probe_grpo/checkpoint-500 \\\n     --data_path /root/mvp_v2_1_expanded/data/calibration_v2_1_expanded.jsonl \\\n     --output_dir /root/contrastive_entrain_calib/results/exp009_ckpt500_eval/ \\\n     --enable_thinking false\n   ```\n   \n   注意：\n   - model_path 可能是 `/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/Qwen/Qwen3-8B` 或 `/root/autodl-tmp/modelscope_cache/Qwen/Qwen3-8B`，先用 `ls` 确认哪个存在\n   - lora_path 用 `--lora_path`（不是 `--adapter_path`）\n   - 如果参数名不对，先 `python eval_unified_preset.py --help` 查看\n\n3. 这个 eval 可能需要 1-2 小时。用 `submit_training_job` 提交以便 cron 监控，或者用 nohup 后台运行并汇报 PID。\n\n   **推荐用 submit_training_job**：\n   - exp_id: exp-009-eval-ckpt500\n   - server: westd-28034\n   - gpu: cuda:3\n   - command: 上面的完整 eval 命令\n\n   如果 submit_training_job 不可用，用 nohup：\n   ```bash\n   nohup bash -c 'CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=3 /root/miniconda3/bin/python evaluation/eval_unified_preset.py --model_path <确认的路径> --lora_path /root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/exp009_continuous_probe_grpo/checkpoint-500 --data_path /root/mvp_v2_1_expanded/data/calibration_v2_1_expanded.jsonl --output_dir /root/contrastive_entrain_calib/results/exp009_ckpt500_eval/ --enable_thinking false' > /root/runs/contrastive_entrain_calib/exp009_ckpt500_eval.log 2>&1 &\n   echo $!\n   ```\n\n4. 汇报：eval 启动成功/失败、PID、预计完成时间\n\n### 环境\n- 服务器: westd-28034, account: root\n- Python: /root/miniconda3/bin/python\n- GPU: cuda:3（空闲）\n- HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1, TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1（无外网）",

    "ssh"

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"worker_id\": \"worker_analysis_exp-012_20260520T072338\", \"status\": \"done\", \"task_type\": \"analysis:exp-012\", \"project\": \"contrastive_entrain_calib\", \"started_at\": \"2026-05-20T07:23:38.220657+00:00\", \"ended_at\": \"2026-05-20T07:25:15.664438+00:00\", \"setup\": null, \"teardown\": null, \"setup_data\": {}, \"result\": {\"raw\": \"Registry 已更新。以下是分析报告：\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 结论\\n**success** — Llama-3-8B CV AUROC = 0.779 > 0.75 阈值，跨模型泛化确认。\\n\\n## 核心指标\\n- **CV AUROC**: 0.7793 ± 0.0189 (5-fold, all folds best at layer 16)\\n- **Full AUROC**: 0.7779\\n- **Best layer**: 16 (Llama 32层模型的第16层，即中间层)\\n- **Style-controlled AUROC**: 0.9227\\n- **Bootstrap CI 95%**: 未单独输出，但 5-fold std=0.019 表明稳定性好\\n\\n## 与 Qwen3-8B 对比\\n\\n| 指标 | Qwen3-8B (exp-001) | Llama-3-8B (exp-012) |\\n|------|-----|-----|\\n| CV AUROC | 0.827 | 0.779 |\\n| Best layer | 19 (of 40) | 16 (of 32) |\\n| Best layer 相对位置 | ~48% depth | ~50% depth |\\n| Style-controlled AUROC | — | 0.923 |\\n| Strong evidence | — | 0.911 |\\n| Weak evidence | — | 0.711 |\\n\\n两个模型的最佳层都在网络中间（~50% depth），模式一致。Llama 绝对 AUROC 低约 0.05，但仍然显著高于 chance (0.5)。\\n\\n## 对论文的意义\\n\\nd_syc 线性方向在两个架构族（Qwen/Llama）上均可提取且 AUROC 显著高于 chance，支持 \\\"discriminability is a general property of instruction-tuned LLMs\\\" 的 claim。这强化了 \\\"discriminability ≠ reward utility\\\" 的核心论点——模型内部能区分 sycophantic vs. faithful responses，但这种信号并未被 reward model 利用。\\n\\n## 意外发现\\n\\n1. **Layer 16 独占最佳**：所有 5 个 fold 最佳层都是 16，与 Qwen 的 layer 19 一样集中在网络中层，但 Llama 的其余层 AUROC 衰减更快（次优层 31 仅 0.747 vs best 0.779），说明 Llama 的 d_syc 信号更集中于单一层。\\n2. **Evidence strength 分层清晰**：strong (0.911) > medium (0.864) > weak (0.711)，说明 d_syc 方向对\\\"更明显的 sycophancy\\\"区分度更高，符合直觉。\\n3. **`std_auroc_per_layer` 字段有 bug**：报告中所有层的 std 值均为 0.743（实际是 fold 4 的 AUROC），应为各层各 fold AUROC 的标准差。不影响最终结果（`best_std_auroc` 字段正确计算为 0.0189），但代码需修复。\"}}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

The analysis worker already updated exp-012 to DONE(success) with all the metrics. Let me now follow the on-experiment-done protocol for success:

2. Update report.md
3. Report to Director with:
   - Success result + comparison to baseline
   - Experiment matrix alignment
   - Reflection on experiment plan
   - Literature linkage
4. Write memory.md entry

Let me now:
1. Report to Director
2. Update memory.md with the key findings


3. Reflect on what this means for the paper and next steps

The results are strong across the board — Llama and Qwen both exceeded the 0.75 threshold with AUROC scores of 0.779 and 0.827 respectively, and the evidence strength gradient shows clear separation between strong, medium, and weak conditions. The style-controlled experiment pushing to 0.923 AUROC is particularly compelling, and the cross-model generalization confirms our core narrative that discriminability doesn't necessarily translate to reward utility. This positions the paper well for the Path B analysis story.

The architectural parallel is striking too — both models converge on the ~50% depth layer as optimal, which strengthens the generalizability claim. The performance gap between Llama and Qwen on the same phenomenon is worth exploring, though it doesn't undermine the findings. Also noting that GPU 0 on node 28034 is now available since exp-012 completed its single-GPU inference run.
```

  "content": "# Memory\n\n## exp-012: Llama-3-8B d_syc 跨模型泛化确认 (2026-05-20)\n- **CV AUROC 0.779 ± 0.019**，超过 0.75 阈值，跨模型泛化成立\n- Best layer: L16/32 (~50% depth)，与 Qwen3-8B L19/40 (~48%) 模式一致\n- Style-controlled AUROC: 0.923（非常高，排除风格混淆）\n- Evidence strength 分层: strong 0.911 > medium 0.864 > weak 0.711\n- Llama d_syc 比 Qwen 略弱（0.779 vs 0.827），但信号更集中于单层（所有 fold 最佳都是 L16）\n- **论文 claim**: \"d_syc generalizes across architectures, concentrated at ~50% network depth\"\n- 模型路径: /root/autodl-tmp/models/LLM-Research/Meta-Llama-3-8B-Instruct\n\n## exp-002v3 Collapse 深度分析：R1 问题是噪声不是幅度 (2026-05-20)\n- **两阶段坍缩**：Phase 1 长度坍缩 (step 100-300, mean_len 247→80) → Phase 2 终末坍缩 (step 2160, entropy 0.056→0.026 in 50 steps)\n- **R3 step 100 即饱和到 0.9998**，提供零学习信号。Combined reward 退化为仅 R1\n- **5x scaling 无效的根因**：cosine R1 mean 全程在 -0.46 ~ +0.41 间剧烈波动，从未出现持续正向趋势。Scaling 同比放大了噪声，信噪比不变\n- **关键洞察**：问题不是 R1 太弱（可以 scale），而是 R1 方差太大（cosine 在高维空间本质上 noisy）。这与 binary probe R1 (exp-008v3) 形成对比：binary 信号虽然也失败（stubbornness trap），但至少信号稳定（mean=+0.46, 100% 正值）\n- **论文影响**：补全 cosine R1 证据链的最后一环。4 个实验（tanh/cosine/recalib/5x scale）都失败，failure mode 一致 = 结构性问题而非工程问题\n\n## D031: Probe Reward Trap — 完整 4-checkpoint trajectory (2026-05-20)\n- **Stubbornness onset: IMMEDIATE (ckpt-500)**，不是渐进过程\n- ckpt-500: ToF=0.000, NoF=0.995, SYCONScore=0.000 — 训练 500 步即 complete stubbornness\n- ckpt-1000: ToF=0.006, NoF=0.995, SYCONScore=0.011 — 微弱回升但本质不变\n- ckpt-1500: ToF=0.000, NoF=1.000, SYCONScore=0.000 — 再次归零\n- ckpt-2000: ToF=0.000, NoF=1.000, SYCONScore=0.000 — 持续\n- **关键洞察**: binary probe R1 在训练最初期就把模型推向\"拒绝所有修改\"。不存在\"先学到有效抵抗再 collapse\"的阶段。这说明 probe reward trap 不是 optimization 问题（如 reward hacking），而是 reward formulation 的结构性问题——probe P(syc)>0.5→R1=0 本质上奖励\"不改变\"而非\"正确地不改变\"\n- **论文价值**: immediate onset 比 gradual collapse 更有说服力——不是超参调优能解决的，是公式层面的根本缺陷\n\n## D029: Story A 死亡，Story B 也失败，转 Path B analysis paper (2026-05-19, updated 2026-05-20)\n- **Story A (continuous cosine R1) 已被证伪**：4/4 实验 negative (tanh/cosine/recalib/5x scale)\n- **Story B (binary probe R1) 也失败**：immediate stubbornness trap\n- **论文转 Path B**: \"discriminability ≠ reward utility\" — d_syc AUROC 0.82 但无论何种 formulation 都无法转化为有效 GRPO reward\n- **exp-002v3 最终结论**: killed at step 2440, two-phase collapse confirmed, R1 noise not magnitude is the root cause\n\n## ⚠️ exp-008v3 mid-eval: 全 4 checkpoint 均 mode collapse (2026-05-20)\n- **eval 方法**: eval_unified_preset.py (D027 标准 preset eval)，参数 --model_path Qwen3-8B --lora_path checkpoint\n- **ckpt-500**: ToF=0.0000, NoF=0.9945, SYCONScore=0.0000 (28034 GPU 2, 6502s)\n- **ckpt-1000**: ToF=0.0055, NoF=0.9945, SYCONScore=0.0110 (28034 GPU 1, 6726s)\n- **ckpt-1500** (entropy 0.61): ToF=0.0000, NoF=0.9945, SYCONScore=0.0000 (29046)\n- **ckpt-2000** (entropy 崩溃后): ToF=0.0000, NoF≈0.99, SYCONScore=0.0000 (29046)\n- **对比 D027 vanilla baseline**: ToF=0.072, NoF=0.945, SYCONScore=0.134\n- **对比 ACT (exp-004)**: SYCONScore=0.301\n- **结论**: 模型在所有 checkpoint 都 100% 拒绝修改答案。Binary probe R1 从训练最初期就推模型走向 complete stubbornness\n- 结果保存: 28034 `/root/contrastive_entrain_calib/results/exp008v3_ckpt{500,1000}_eval/`, 29046 `/root/autodl-tmp/results/exp008v3_ckpt{1500,2000}_eval/`\n\n## exp002v2-recalib 最终结果 (2026-05-20, step 4000 crashed)\n- **总步数**: 3500 步（step 501→4000），在 step 4000 保存 checkpoint 时磁盘写满崩溃\n- **终止原因**: `PytorchStreamWriter failed writing file data/293` — 根分区磁盘空间不足\n- **Recalibration 追踪**（每 500 步，共 7 次）：\n  - Step 500: cos_sim=0.670, AUROC=0.815, d_norm=79.1\n  - Step 1000: cos_sim=0.860, AUROC=0.855, d_norm=91.1\n  - Step 1500: cos_sim=0.762, AUROC=0.855, d_norm=90.3\n  - Step 2000: cos_sim=0.799, AUROC=0.847, d_norm=84.5\n  - Step 2500: cos_sim=0.850, AUROC=0.900, d_norm=142.8\n  - Step 3000: cos_sim=0.936, AUROC=0.903, d_norm=120.6\n  - Step 3500: cos_sim=0.858, AUROC=0.909, d_norm=164.1\n- **Recalibration 有效性**: AUROC 从 0.82 提升到 0.91（方向质量改善），但 d_norm 膨胀 79→164\n- **Conclusion: NEGATIVE** — mode collapse 确认\n- **证伪**: periodic recalibration 能维持方向质量（AUROC↑）但无法修复 cosine R1 结构性负值\n\n## Cosine R1 结构性失效 — 完整证据链 (2026-05-19, updated 2026-05-20)\n- **exp-002v2-recalib (4000 steps)**: R1 mean=-0.12，entropy=0.057（mode collapse），recalibration AUROC 0.82→0.91 但 R1 始终负\n- **exp-002v3 (scale×5, killed step 2440)**: 两阶段坍缩。R3 step 100 饱和 0.9998，R1 mean 在 -0.46~+0.41 剧烈波动无趋势。问题是 R1 噪声不是幅度\n- **R3 完全饱和（>0.998）**：combined reward 被 R3 主导，R1 梯度被淹没\n- **Recalibration 无法解决**：方向质量提升了（AUROC 0.82→0.91），但 cosine 公式本身无法产生正向 reward\n- **对比 Binary Probe R1**：exp-008v3 R1 mean=+0.46 (100% 正值) vs cosine R1 mean=-0.064~-0.129\n- **论文结论**：continuous cosine formulation structurally fails; binarization is necessary to convert representation-level signal into effective RL reward\n- **Story A AUROC=0.8166 vs multi-layer(7-dim) AUROC=0.8349，差距仅 +0.018\n- **Ablation B (random-direction probe)**: random 7-dim AUROC=0.7265 ±0.017 vs d_syc 7-dim AUROC=0.8349，差距 +0.108\n- **结论**: d_syc 方向质量贡献 (+0.108) 远大于多层组合贡献 (+0.018)\n\n## EMNLP 2026 投稿信息 (2026-05-19)\n- **目标会议**：EMNLP 2026, Budapest, 10月22-26日\n- **投稿途径**：全部走 ARR May cycle，无 direct submission\n- **ARR 提交截止**：2026-05-25 (AoE)\n- **Commitment deadline**：2026-08-02\n\n## D027: 统一评估指标标准 (2026-05-19, 修正 2026-05-20)\n- **废弃 WRR**：永久弃用，不再报告\n- **主报指标**：ToF / NoF / SYCONScore = harmonic_mean(ToF, NoF)\n- **分类方法**：NLI-based（DeBERTa cross-encoder）\n- **D027 Preset Eval vanilla baseline**: ToF=0.072, NoF=0.945, SYCONScore=0.134\n- **统一 eval 脚本**：eval_unified_preset.py（实际路径 eval/eval_unified_preset.py）\n- **eval 参数注意**: 用 (2026-05-18)\n- `/root/miniconda3/bin/` 不在默认 PATH 中，tmux 必须用绝对路径\n- Python: `/root/miniconda3/bin/python` (3.12)\n- DeepSpeed `--include localhost:X,Y` 替代 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES\n- 无外网，加载需 `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 && TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1`\n- Qwen3-8B: `/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/Qwen/Qwen3-8B` 或 `/root/autodl-tmp/modelscope_cache/Qwen/Qwen3-8B`\n- OUTPUT_DIR 必须在 `/root/autodl-tmp/`（根分区仅 {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"worker_id\": \"worker_paper_writing_cosine_failure_20260520T074932\", \"status\": \"done\", \"task_type\": \"paper_writing:cosine_failure\", \"project\": \"contrastive_entrain_calib\", \"started_at\": \"2026-05-20T07:49:33.018383+00:00\", \"ended_at\": \"2026-05-20T07:51:03.270988+00:00\", \"setup\": null, \"teardown\": null, \"setup_data\": {}, \"result\": {\"raw\": \"§4 已写入 `docs/paper/sections/cosine_failure.tex`，约 700 words。\\n\\n结构：\\n- **开头**：从 §3 的 discriminability 结果过渡，预告 4-experiment diagnostic chain\\n- **Table 1** (`tab:cosine_chain`)：4 行总结 F1a--F1d 的配置、公式、outcome、root cause\\n- **F1a**：tanh gate saturation，norm mismatch 导致梯度消失\\n- **F1b**：raw cosine gap 仅 0.098，信号被 R3 方差淹没\\n- **F1c**：recalibration 把 AUROC 提到 0.91 但引入 reward discontinuity，导致 mode collapse\\n- **F1d**：5x scaling 放大 noise，R3 step 100 饱和后 R1 独撑但 oscillate，终端 collapse\\n- **Unified diagnosis**：cosine projection 把 warranted revision 和 unwarranted capitulation 混为一谈，方向编码 belief change direction 但不编码 warrant；过渡到 §5 binary probe\"}}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}
```

- 如果 ToF > 0 → 报告结果，继续观察

**任务 2 [高优先级]: 28034 GPU 0 — 启动 Llama probe ablation**
exp-012 已完成，GPU 0 空闲。利用已有的 Llama-3-8B 激活数据，跑 probe ablation：
- 在 Llama 激活上训练 binary probe（复用 exp-008 的 probe 训练代码，换成 Llama 激活）
- 对比 d_syc direction probe AUROC vs random direction probe AUROC
- 目标：验证 Llama 上 d_syc 的 probe ablation gap 是否与 Qwen 一致（Qwen: 0.835 vs 0.727 = +0.108）
- 这个分析 ~1-2h，不需要训练 GRPO，纯推理+probe fitting

所有 ssh_read_only 任务可以并行。GPU 0 的 Llama probe ablation 写代码后先 dry-run 再正式跑。

{
  "task_type": "code:llama-probe-ablation",
  "project": "contrastive_entrain_calib",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：在 28034 GPU 0 上编写并运行 Llama probe ablation 实验\n\n### 背景\nexp-012 已完成，在 Llama-3-8B-Instruct 上提取了 L16-L31 的 hidden states，结果保存在 `/root/contrastive_entrain_calib/results/exp012_llama_direction/`。现在需要做 probe ablation，验证 Llama 上 d_syc 方向的 probe ablation gap 是否与 Qwen 一致。\n\nQwen 的 probe ablation 结果：d_syc probe AUROC=0.835 vs random direction probe AUROC=0.727，gap=+0.108。\n\n### 你要做的\n\n1. SSH 到 westd-28034，查看 exp-012 的输出文件：\n   ```bash\n   ls -la /root/contrastive_entrain_calib/results/exp012_llama_direction/\n   cat /root/contrastive_entrain_calib/results/exp012_llama_direction/evaluation_report.json 2>/dev/null | head -50\n   ```\n\n2. 查看 Qwen 的 probe ablation 代码（参考实现）：\n   ```bash\n   # 查找 probe ablation 相关代码\n   find /root/contrastive_entrain_calib/ -name \"*ablation*\" -o -name \"*probe*\" | grep -v __pycache__ | head -20\n   ```\n\n3. 编写 `run_llama_probe_ablation.py` 脚本，放到 `/root/contrastive_entrain_calib/training/`：\n   - 加载 exp-012 保存的 Llama activations（`activations.pt` 或类似文件）\n   - 加载 exp-012 保存的 d_syc 方向（`directions.pt` 或类似文件）\n   - 训练 logistic regression probe on d_syc direction features（投影到 d_syc 方向后的标量值）\n   - 训练 logistic regression probe on random direction features（随机方向投影）\n   - 重复 random 100 次取均值和 std\n   - 5-fold CV AUROC 对比\n   - 保存结果到 `/root/contrastive_entrain_calib/results/exp012_llama_probe_ablation/`\n\n4. Dry-run 验证脚本无语法错误：\n   ```bash\n   /root/miniconda3/bin/python /root/contrastive_entrain_calib/training/run_llama_probe_ablation.py --help 2>&1 | head -5\n   ```\n\n5. 正式运行（纯 CPU + 小量 sklearn 计算，不需要 GPU，但加载 activations 可能需要 GPU 内存）：\n   ```bash\n   CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 /root/miniconda3/bin/python /root/contrastive_entrain_calib/training/run_llama_probe_ablation.py\n   ```\n   这个应该很快（<30min），等结果出来直接汇报。\n\n6. 汇报结果：\n   - Llama d_syc probe AUROC\n   - Llama random probe AUROC (mean ± std)\n   - Gap\n   - 与 Qwen 对比\n\n### 代码规范\n- 文件顶部注释说明用途\n- 结果保存为 JSON\n\n### 环境\n- 服务器: westd-28034, account: root\n- Python: /root/miniconda3/bin/python\n- sklearn 应已安装，如果没有先 pip install",
  "tool_groups": [
    "ssh"
  ]
}

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Excellent results from the Llama probe ablation! Key findings:

- Llama d_syc probe AUROC: 0.813 (multi-layer) / 0.778 (best single layer L16)
- Llama random direction AUROC: 0.530 ± 0.054
- Gap: +0.248 (vs Qwen's +0.108)

This is a very interesting finding - the gap is actually LARGER on Llama. The random direction baseline on Llama is near chance (0.53), meaning Llama's sycophancy signal is more concentrated in specific directions, while Qwen has more diffuse signal in the activation space.


Now I need to update the registry with these probe ablation metrics for exp-012, report the findings to the Director, and check on the status of exp-009 since that's the highest priority item.
```

_source line 471 · 2026-05-20T08:29:47.419Z_

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   This is an ML research project (contrastive_entrain_calib) targeting EMNLP 2026, investigating whether internal sycophancy direction (d_syc) in LLMs can be converted to effective GRPO reward signals. The paper has pivoted to "Path B analysis paper" arguing "discriminability ≠ reward utility" — d_syc has AUROC 0.82 but all reward formulations (cosine, scaled cosine, binary probe, continuous probe) fail structurally.

   The session started with a failed ghost-process worker callback, triggering session initialization. Director then issued multiple urgent instructions:
   - Kill exp-002v3 (collapsed) and start Llama-3-8B Phase A (exp-012)
   - Full status checks across both servers
   - Launch exp-009 ckpt-500 D027 eval
   - Start Llama probe ablation
   - Check literature_map rebuild status
   - Paper writing (multiple sections being written in parallel by workers)

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **DC-GRPO**: Direction-Contrastive Group Relative Policy Optimization — using contrastive activation directions as RL reward signals
   - **d_syc**: Linear sycophancy direction extracted via mean-difference from contrastive activation pairs
   - **R1 formulations**: cosine (F1), scaled cosine 5x (F1d), binary probe (F3), continuous probe probability (F2) — all fail
   - **AUROC**: Area Under ROC curve for direction quality measurement (Qwen: 0.827, Llama: 0.779)
   - **SYCONScore**: harmonic_mean(ToF, NoF) — primary evaluation metric (D027 standard)
   - **ToF**: Turn of Flip (valid correction acceptance rate)
   - **NoF**: No Flip (invalid pressure resistance rate)
   - **Mode collapse**: entropy → 0, grad_norm → 0, frac_reward_zero_std → 1
   - **Probe reward trap**: binary probe R1 rewards "not changing" rather than "correctly not changing" → immediate stubbornness
   - **DeepSpeed ZeRO-2**: distributed training framework used for multi-GPU experiments
   - **ModelScope**: Chinese CDN alternative to HuggingFace for model downloads on AutoDL servers
   - **Path B analysis paper**: "discriminability ≠ reward utility" narrative
   - **Probe ablation**: comparing d_syc direction probe vs random direction probe AUROC

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **`/root/contrastive_entrain_calib/training/run_exp012_llama_direction.py`** (488 lines, on westd-28034)
     - Llama-3-8B-Instruct direction extraction script
     - Loads calibration_v2_1_expanded.jsonl (362 samples, 181 pairs)
     - Forward pass with apply_chat_template, extracts L16-L31 hidden states (last token)
     - Computes d_syc via mean-diff, 5-fold stratified CV AUROC, style-controlled AUROC
     - Saves directions.pt, activations.pt, evaluation_report.json
   
   - **`/root/contrastive_entrain_calib/training/run_llama_probe_ablation.py`** (on westd-28034)
     - Probe ablation analysis using exp-012 saved activations
     - Logistic regression probe on d_syc direction vs 10x random directions
     - Results: d_syc 0.813 vs random 0.530±0.054, gap +0.248

   - **`docs/paper/sections/framework.tex`** (§2, ~730 words)
     - Direction extraction, GRPO reward formulation, recalibration, evaluation protocol

   - **`docs/paper/sections/direction_quality.tex`** (§3)
     - Qwen/Llama AUROC comparison, evidence strength gradient, probe ablation, training stability

   - **`docs/paper/sections/cosine_failure.tex`** (§4, ~700 words)
     - 4-experiment cosine R1 diagnostic chain (F1a-F1d), unified diagnosis

   - **`docs/paper/sections/probe_trap.tex`** (§5, ~660 words)
     - Differentiable probe gaming, binary probe stubbornness trap, warrant encoding gap

   - **`artifacts/figures/fig_layer_auroc.pdf/png`**
     - Cross-model layer-wise AUROC figure

   - **`memory.md`** — Updated multiple times with experiment insights, environment notes
   - **`report.md`** — Auto-generated handover document
   - **`ROADMAP.md`** — Research dashboard, Path B analysis paper direction
   - **`literature_map.yaml`** — 21 papers, all verified, 5 categories

4. Errors and Fixes:
   - **Ghost process worker failed** (worker_ops_fix-ghost-process-29046): No output available. Investigation revealed no ghost process existed on 29046 — false alarm.
   - **exp-009 cron missing**: Initially thought missing (not in reconcile output), but `register_cron` revealed it was already registered. `poll_cron` confirmed working.
   - **exp-002v3 mode collapse**: entropy=0.009, grad_norm≈0 at step 2440. Root cause: R3 saturated at step 100, cosine R1 noise too high (variance, not magnitude). Killed per Director instruction.
   - **exp-012 model not downloaded**: Llama-3-8B-Instruct not on server. Downloaded via ModelScope (~15GB, ~2.5h at 1.8MB/s). Path: `/root/autodl-tmp/models/LLM-Research/Meta-Llama-3-8B-Instruct`
   - **exp-012 script --layers parameter**: Script doesn't support `--layers` CLI arg; layer selection hardcoded internally. Worker adapted by removing the argument.
   - **eval script path**: Actual path is `eval/eval_unified_preset.py` (not `evaluation/`). No `--enable_thinking` parameter exists.
   - **exp-003 log stale 10h+**: Confirmed caused by tee pipe buffering, not training stuck. Process alive, ckpt-4500 eventually appeared.

5. Problem Solving:
   - **exp-002v3 collapse diagnosed**: Two-phase collapse (length collapse step 200, terminal collapse step 2160). R3 early saturation leaves only noisy R1 signal. 5x scaling amplifies noise proportionally — SNR unchanged.
   - **Cross-model generalization confirmed**: Llama CV AUROC 0.779 > 0.75 threshold, best layer at ~50% depth matching Qwen pattern.
   - **Probe ablation gap larger on Llama**: +0.248 vs Qwen's +0.108. Random direction near chance (0.53) on Llama vs 0.73 on Qwen — sycophancy signal more concentrated in specific directions on Llama.
   - **GPU utilization**: Achieved 6/6 GPU saturation across both servers.

        "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/agent-ml-research/skills/global/resource-saturation.skill.md",
        "content": "---\nname: resource-saturation\ndescription: \"当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。\"\nalways: false\nroles:\n  - director\n  - agent\n---\n\n# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）\n\n## A. GPU 分配率检查\n\n调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:\n- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号\n- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）\n- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️\n\n任何\"分配了但没跑\"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:\n- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备\n- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）\n- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报\"暂无可铺实验\"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作\n\n**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。\n\n## B. GPU 效率检查\n\n调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:\n- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务\n- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）\n- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优\n\n## C. 非 GPU 任务并行\n\nGPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:\n- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint\n- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置\n- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索\n- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合\n- 论文章节起草\n- 代码 review / refactor\n\n**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。\n\n## D. 汇报格式\n\n向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:\n\n    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)\n",

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-be-idea-gen-0515-3/projects/contrastive_entrain_calib/report.md\", \"content\": \"# Handover — 2026-05-20\\n\\n> 由 Agent 生成，每次状态变更后自动更新。硬限 150 行。\\n\\n## 运行中\\n| exp_id | 假设 | 状态 | 关键指标 | W&B |\\n|--------|------|------|---------|-----|\\n| exp-009 | Continuous Probe Probability GRPO: R1 = 1 - P(sycophantic|h) using frozen probe's sigmoid output as continuous reward signal. This is the intermediate formulation between cosine R1 (pure continuous, structural failure due to conflating all changes) and binary probe R1 (pure discrete, immediate stubbornness trap). If continuous probability also fails, it completes the \\\"ALL representation-to-reward formulations fail\\\" argument — continuous/probability/binary all structurally cannot convert d_syc discriminability into effective RL reward. If it succeeds, continuous probe probability is a viable middle ground. | — | — | — |\\n| exp-003-s2 | R3-only GRPO baseline (seed 2): training with only the external DeBERTa reward (r3_weight=1.0, r1=r2=0) provides a lower bound for ablation, isolating the contribution of contrastive direction-based rewards (R1+R2). Seed 2 (seed=43) verifies reproducibility of seed 1 results. | — | — | — |\\n| exp-003 | R3-only GRPO (NLI behavioral reward without direction-contrastive R1/R2) will reduce sycophantic capitulation but less effectively than full DC-GRPO, establishing the behavioral-only baseline for Claim 2 (DC-GRPO effectiveness) | — | — | — |\\n\\n## 待处理\\n- exp-009-eval-ckpt500（准备中）\\n\\n## 近期结论\\n- **exp-012** (success): If Llama-3-8B hidden states (L16-L33) contain a linear sycophancy direction with AUROC >= 0.75, then discriminability generalizes beyond Qwen3-8B, strengthening the discriminability != reward utility claim. ← 基于 exp-001\\n- **exp-002v3** (negative): DC-GRPO with scaled cosine R1 (scale_factor=5, r1_weight=0.4, r3_weight=0.6). Cosine similarity in 4096-dim space is inherently small (gap ~0.098), contributing only ~8% of combined reward at default scale. Scaling by 5x amplifies R1 gap to ~0.49, making R1 contribute ~45% of combined reward alongside R3. If scaled R1 shows learning trend and improves sycophancy reduction over R3-only (exp-003), this validates that direction-projection provides meaningful training signal when properly scaled.\\n- **exp-008v3** (negative): Binary Probe GRPO: frozen linear probe output binarized (P(syc)>0.5→R1=0, else R1=1) as GRPO R1 reward signal. Detached probe with stop-gradient prevents adversarial gaming (unlike exp-008 where continuous probe was gamed in ~30 steps). If Binary Probe GRPO ≈ DC-GRPO, direction projection adds no value beyond binary classification; if DC-GRPO > Binary, continuous directional information is the critical differentiator. ← 基于 exp-008\\n\\n## 趋势\\n成功 4 / 负向 4 / 失败 0 / 共 12\\n\\n## 下一步\\n（无活跃 idea，请通过 ideas.md 添加）\\n\\n## 资源状态\\nwestd-28034: 无 GPU 信息\\nwestd-29046: 无 GPU 信息\\n\\n## 告警\\n- exp-002: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp-008: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp-014: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp-002v2: 已被终止 — unknown\\n\", \"total_chars\": 2855, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}
```

    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

  "parentUuid": "d5c8558a-4dfc-4a30-98ce-44cdbd3a0f64",

  "attachment": {
    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

\\\"gpu_budget_hours\\\": 55, \\\"deps\\\": [\\\"exp-001\\\", \\\"exp-002\\\"], \\\"status\\\": \\\"planned\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"从 P2 升级到 P1。Phase A 仅 5h（方向计算+AUROC），Phase B 需 ~50h。 Phase B 复用 condition（无施压的正常对话），从 warranted_revision vs neutral 计算独立 d_gen（与 d_syc 正交）。打破 d_gen = -d_syc 限制， 恢复 R2 为独立信号。\\\\n\\\", \\\"priority\\\": \\\"P2\\\", \\\"gpu_budget_hours\\\": 5, \\\"deps\\\": [\\\"exp-001\\\"], \\\"status\\\": \\\"planned\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"需扩展校准数据生成器。如果 exp-002 主实验效果已足够好则降低优先级。\\\"}, {\\\"id\\\": \\\"exp-014\\\", \\\"registry_id\\\": \\\"exp-014\\\", \\\"name\\\": \\\"continuous_probe_baseline\\\", \\\"claim\\\": 2, \\\"description\\\": \\\"Continuous Probe Baseline：用 logistic regression 概率值（而非 binary 输出） 作为 reward 信号。隔离\\\\\\\"方向性 vs 连续性\\\\\\\"对 DC-GRPO 相对 Binary Probe 优势的贡献。 如果 continuous probe ≈ DC-GRPO 效果，则 DC-GRPO 的优势来自连续信号而非方向几何。 这是论文 framing 的存亡级判据（D012）。\\\\n\\\", \\\"priority\\\": \\\"P0\\\", \\\"gpu_budget_hours\\\": 20, \\\"deps\\\": Logistic regression 在 calibration data 上训练，输出概率作为 R1 替代。\\\\n\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"killed\\\", \\\"metrics\\\": {\\\"preset_eval_ckpt3000\\\": {\\\"ToF\\\": 0.978, \\\"NoF\\\": 0.006, ActAdd improves NoF but destroys ToF, net negative under NLI-based SYCONScore.\\\", \\\"conclusion_reason\\\": \\\"D027 eval reversal: ActAdd alpha=5 SYCONScore 0.054 worse than vanilla 0.134\\\", \\\"unified_reeval_D027\\\": {\\\"alpha_0.0\\\": {\\\"ToF\\\": 0.0718, \\\"NoF\\\": 0.9448, \\\"SYCONScore\\\": 0.1335}, \\\"alpha_0.5\\\": {\\\"ToF\\\": 0.0718, \\\"NoF\\\": 0.9392, \\\"SYCONScore\\\": 0.1334}, \\\"alpha_1.0\\\": {\\\"ToF\\\": 0.0442, \\\"NoF\\\": 0.9724, \\\"SYCONScore\\\": 0.0846}, \\\"alpha_2.0\\\": {\\\"ToF\\\": 0.0276, \\\"NoF\\\": 0.9669, \\\"SYCONScore\\\": 0.0537}, \\\"alpha_5.0\\\": {\\\"ToF\\\": 0.0276, \\\"NoF\\\": 0.9613, \\\"SYCONScore\\\": 0.0537}, \\\"eval_server\\\": \\\"westd-28034\\\", \\\"eval_results_path\\\": \\\"/root/contrastive_entrain_calib/results/exp016_unified_reeval/\\\"}}}], \\\"total\\\": 16}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {}}\"}",

\"notes\": \"MVP v2.1 仅 50 pairs 全 weak evidence，σ=0.054 太大。 扩至 200 pairs（80 weak + 60 medium + 60 strong）可显著降低方差。 API 成本 ~$10-20，GPU 仅 forward pass。\\n\", \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"metrics\": SSH 到 westd-29046 (ssh_read_only)，检查两个进程存活：\n   ```bash\n   ps -p 70649,237432 -o pid,stat,etime 2>/dev/null\n   ```\n\n2. 检查 exp-003 训练进度：\n   ```bash\n   # 查最新 checkpoint\n   ls -lt /root/contrastive_entrain_calib/outputs/exp003_r3only/checkpoint-* 2>/dev/null | head -5\n   # tail 日志\n   tail -30 /root/contrastive_entrain_calib/outputs/exp003_r3only/training.log 2>/dev/null || tail -30 /root/runs/contrastive_entrain_calib/exp-003*.log 2>/dev/null\n   ```\n\n3. 检查 exp-003-s2 训练进度：\n   ```bash\n   ls -lt /root/contrastive_entrain_calib/outputs/exp003_r3only_s2/checkpoint-* 2>/dev/null | head -5\n   tail -30 /root/contrastive_entrain_calib/outputs/exp003_r3only_s2/training.log 2>/dev/null || tail -30 /root/runs/contrastive_entrain_calib/exp-003-s2*.log 2>/dev/null\n   ```\n\n4. 用 nvidia-smi 确认 GPU 利用率：\n   ```bash\n   nvidia-smi --query-gpu=index,utilization.gpu,memory.used,memory.total --format=csv,noheader\n   ```\n\n### 产出\n- 两个实验的当前 step / 总 step\n- 预计完成时间 (ETA)\n- GPU 利用率\n- 任何异常（log error、进程状态异常等）\n",

Key findings:
- **exp-003**: step ~5000/8000 (62.5%), ETA May 21 ~18:00 CST, ~25 hours remaining. Log stopped at step 3760 but checkpoints and process are fine.
- **exp-003-s2**: step ~845/8000 (10.6%), ETA May 23 ~05:00 CST, ~60 hours remaining.
- Both GPUs running well (89-91% utilization)
- Interesting: clipped_ratio=1.0, all completions truncated at max_length=256. This might be worth noting.


- The truncation issue (clipped_ratio=1.0) is affecting training quality since completions never naturally end, but this is a known baseline limitation for R3-only experiments, not specific to this run.

The worker pool dropped to zero after the last task finished. I need to maintain at least 2 active workers, so I'm planning to spawn CPU-based tasks like paper section work while I wait for the Director's response on GPU 0 allocation.

Looking at what still needs to be written for the paper, I have the framework, direction quality, cosine failure, and probe trap sections done, plus the introduction. What's missing is Related Work, Discussion/Conclusion, Abstract, and Results (which depends on exp-003 finishing). I should spawn workers to update the ROADMAP with the current experiment status and tackle one of the missing paper sections—probably Related Work since it doesn't depend on experimental results.

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-be-idea-gen-0515-3/projects/contrastive_entrain_calib/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — contrastive_entrain_calib\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n- Primary: **EMNLP 2026** via ARR May 2026 cycle\\n- ARR Submission Deadline: **May 25, 2026** (6 days from May 19)\\n- Commitment Deadline: **August 2, 2026**\\n- ⚠️ May cycle 新政策：提交时必须声明目标会议为 EMNLP，绑定不可更改\\n- Fallback: ARR August 2026 cycle (DDL ~Aug 15) → EACL 2027 / ACL 2027\\n- Stretch: ICLR 2027 (abstract ~Sep 2026, full paper ~Oct 2026)\\n\\n## Core Claims (3 条, 可证伪)\\n\\n1. **Directional discriminability + training stability**: The policy model's activation space encodes a linear direction d_syc that discriminates sycophantic capitulation from genuine belief revision (AUROC 0.82), with signal emerging at mid-layers (L16-19). During GRPO training, d_syc remains stable (cosine ≈ 0.96 at step 500), and linear separability is preserved (AUROC 0.80 at step 500). Periodic recalibration (every 500 steps) is included as a safety mechanism.\\n   - *Evidence needed*: AUROC on style-controlled data, layer progression, direction stability curve, AUROC at each checkpoint\\n   - *MVP basis*: v2.1 CV AUROC 0.826, Layer 0 = random, L16-19 emergence\\n   - *New finding (D025 corrects D024)*: Initial stability measurement reported cosine=0.28 (74° drift) but this was a measurement artifact from referencing a stale directions.pt file (different dataset). Actual drift: cosine ≈ 0.96. Direction is stable during training.\\n   - *New finding (D019)*: Per-layer cosine AUROC ≥ 0.77 at step 500, deep layers L32/33 improved +0.025/+0.033. Full training curve (exp-006) pending.\\n   - *New finding (D036)*: **Cross-model generalization confirmed.** Llama-3-8B-Instruct 5-fold CV AUROC=0.7793±0.019 (best layer=16), style-controlled=0.9227. Same strength gradient as Qwen3-8B (weak 0.711 < medium 0.864 < strong 0.911). d_syc is a cross-model phenomenon, not architecture-specific. This strengthens the negative claim: even cross-model discriminability fails as reward.\\n   - *New finding (D025→D029)*: **Cosine R1 structural failure** — cosine(h, d_syc) gap between valid/invalid is ~0.098 (7-layer mean), far too small for GRPO reward. Four experiments confirmed this is structural, not tunable: exp-002 (tanh gate), exp-002v2 (raw cosine), exp002v2-recalib (mode collapse), exp-002v3 (5x scale, strong evidence samples get R1=-0.305 i.e. ALL negative). d_syc conflates legitimate revision with sycophantic capitulation at the continuous projection level. Binarization via probe resolves this (exp-008v3 R1 mean +0.72).\\n\\n2. **Discriminability ≠ reward utility (D031, core finding)**: d_syc direction successfully discriminates sycophancy (AUROC 0.82) but FAILS as GRPO reward in ALL tested forms — continuous cosine, scaled cosine, and binarized probe. The gap between discriminability and reward utility is the paper's central finding.\\n   - *Evidence*:\\n     - **Cosine R1 failure chain** (4 experiments): exp-002 (tanh gate collapse), exp-002v2 (R1≈0), exp002v2-recalib (mode collapse), exp-002v3 (5x scale, strong evidence R1=-0.305 all negative)\\n     - **Binary probe R1 failure** (exp-008v3): ckpt-1500 ToF=0.0000, ckpt-2000 ToF=0.0000. Probe reward trap: model learns \\\"never change answer\\\" → probe rewards as \\\"non-sycophantic\\\" → positive feedback loop → stubbornness collapse\\n     - **Probe ablation (D030)**: d_syc probe AUROC 0.835 vs random 0.727 (+0.108) — direction HAS signal, but signal cannot be converted to effective reward\\n   - *Unified root cause*: d_syc encodes belief change DIRECTION but not change WARRANT. Cosine R1 penalizes ALL change (too punitive), binary probe R1 rewards ALL non-change (too permissive). Both fail because the reward signal conflates desired and degenerate behavior.\\n   - *Recalibration evidence*: AUROC improves during training (0.82→0.91 via recalibration) yet reward still fails → direction QUALITY is not the bottleneck, reward FORMULATION is.\\n\\n3. **Probe reward trap mechanism (D031, strengthened D032)**: Binary probe trained on sycophancy contrastive pairs learns \\\"no answer change = non-sycophantic.\\\" During GRPO training, this creates a positive feedback loop: stubbornness → high R1 → gradient reinforces stubbornness → entropy collapse. The trap is **immediate**: behavioral collapse (ToF=0) occurs within the first 500 training steps, while entropy remains healthy (0.70) until step ~1840 — a **1300-step lag** between behavioral and distributional collapse.\\n   - *Evidence (full checkpoint trajectory)*:\\n     - ckpt-500: entropy=0.70 (healthy), **ToF=0.0000** — trap already active\\n     - ckpt-1000: entropy=0.80, ToF=0.0055 (1/181, noise)\\n     - ckpt-1500: entropy=0.61, ToF=0.0000\\n     - ckpt-2000: entropy=0.30 (collapsed), ToF=0.0000, NoF=1.0000\\n   - *Timeline reconstruction*: Step 0-500: probe rewards stubbornness → behavior dies. Step 500-1840: behavior dead but policy distribution still diverse (model expresses stubbornness in varied ways). Step 1840+: distribution also collapses → expression fixed.\\n   - *Methodological insight*: Entropy-based early stopping would NOT have caught this failure — entropy was 0.70 when ToF was already 0. Behavioral eval at intermediate checkpoints is necessary. R1 mean +0.72 during collapse = probe actively rewarding degenerate behavior.\\n   - *Proposed figure*: Behavioral vs Distributional Collapse dual-axis plot (ToF + entropy over 4 checkpoints), showing 1300-step lag.\\n   - *Theoretical implication*: Any reward derived from d_syc (without conditioning on input validity) will exhibit one of two failure modes — penalizing all change OR rewarding all non-change. Future work needs condition-aware reward design that jointly encodes change direction AND change warrant.\\n\\n## Story Spine\\n\\n**Problem**: LLMs face a \\\"double-bind\\\" in multi-turn dialogue. Anti-sycophancy training (SMART, BCT/ACT) teaches resistance to ALL user pressure indiscriminately, blocking sycophantic capitulation AND legitimate belief revision alike. Meanwhile, multi-turn commitment compounds the problem: TRUTH DECAY shows up to 47% accuracy degradation as models lock into wrong\\n\\n... Limitations outside page limit.\\n  deadline: \\\"August cycle (~Aug 15)\\\"\\n  core_claim: >\\n    A linear sycophancy direction (d_syc) achieves AUROC 0.82 (Qwen3-8B) and 0.78\\n    (Llama-3-8B), demonstrating cross-model discriminability, yet fails as GRPO reward\\n    in ALL tested formulations — continuous cosine penalizes all belief change while\\n    binarized probe rewards all resistance — both exploiting the direction's blindness\\n    to change warrant. We identify a probe reward trap causing behavioral collapse\\n    1300 steps before entropy collapse, revealing that discriminability ≠ reward utility\\n    in representation-guided RL.\\n  working_title: \\\"Discriminability Without Utility: Why Sycophancy Directions Fail as RL Rewards\\\"\\n  paper_type: analysis  # negative result + mechanistic analysis\\n  created_at: 2026-05-20T10:00:00Z\\n\\n# ============================================================\\n# PAPER OUTLINE\\n# ============================================================\\npaper_outline:\\n  - section: abstract\\n    word_budget: 200\\n    core_bullets:\\n      - \\\"Problem: anti-sycophancy training faces a double-bind — blocking capitulation also blocks legitimate revision. Representation-based approaches promise selective targeting.\\\"\\n      - \\\"Setup: extract d_syc from contrastive pairs (AUROC 0.82), test three R1 formulations as GRPO reward\\\"\\n      - \\\"Negative result: ALL formulations fail. Cosine R1 penalizes all change (strong-evidence R1=-0.305). Binary probe R1 rewards all non-change (ToF=0 within 500 steps).\\\"\\n      - \\\"Mechanism: d_syc encodes belief change direction but not warrant → probe reward trap → behavioral collapse precedes entropy collapse by ~1300 steps\\\"\\n      - \\\"Implication: discriminability ≠ reward utility; condition-aware reward design needed\\\"\\n    dependencies: [introduction, cosine_failure, probe_trap, discussion]\\n\\n  - section: introduction\\n    word_budget: 900\\n    core_bullets:\\n      - \\\"Hook: RepE-style directions can detect sycophancy with AUROC 0.82 in LLM activations. A natural next step is using these directions as RL reward signals. We show this fails — the gap between detecting a behavior and penalizing it is fundamental.\\\"\\n      - \\\"Gap: existing anti-sycophancy methods (ACT, behavioral GRPO) treat all belief changes uniformly. RepE/CRAFT/Latent-GRPO use representation geometry as reward for reasoning and safety, but sycophancy's unique geometric structure (capitulation ≈ revision in direction space) remains uninvestigated.\\\"\\n      - \\\"Insight: d_syc encodes belief change direction but not change warrant. The same geometric direction captures both 'model correctly revises belief' and 'model sycophantically capitulates.'\\\"\\n      - \\\"Contributions: (C1) cross-model validated d_syc discriminability (Qwen3-8B AUROC 0.82, Llama-3-8B AUROC 0.78) with systematic 3-formulation investigation establishing discriminability ≠ reward utility; (C2) probe reward trap mechanism with behavioral-before-distributional collapse diagnostic; (C3) unified root cause explaining why sycophancy is geometrically harder than reasoning/safety for representation-guided RL\\\"\\n    dependencies: [cosine_failure, probe_trap]\\n    figures: [fig1_overview]\\n    citation_hints:\\n      - \\\"RepE (Zou et al. 2023), CCS (Burns et al. 2023), ActAdd (Turner et al. 2023)\\\"\\n      - \\\"CRAFT / Latent-GRPO for representation-guided RL comparison\\\"\\n      - \\\"Sycophancy definition and prevalence (Sharma et al. 2023)\\\"\\n    registry_refs: [exp-001, exp-012]\\n\\n  - section: framework\\n    latex_title: \\\"Experimental Framework\\\"\\n    word_budget: 750\\n    core_bullets:\\n      - \\\"§2.1 Direction extraction: contrastive pairs (valid correction vs sycophantic capitulation), mean-difference d_syc across target layers, style-controlled to isolate cognitive signal from surface patterns\\\"\\n      - \\\"§2.2 GRPO reward: R = w1·R1(direction-based) + w3·R3(NLI behavioral). Three R1 formulations — (F1) cosine: cos(h, d_syc), (F2) scaled cosine: α·cos(h, d_syc), (F3) binary probe: I[P(syc|h)<0.5]\\\"\\n      - \\\"§2.3 Online recalibration: d_syc re-extracted every 500 steps (empirical stability cosine ≈ 0.96)\\\"\\n      - \\\"§2.4 Evaluation protocol: ToF (acceptance of valid corrections ↑), NoF (resistance to invalid pressure ↑), SYCONScore = 2·ToF·NoF/(ToF+NoF).
